**Owner's latest evidence:** They sent a full-screen v70 Library screenshot after our WS16BK bookcase navigation APK. The image shows the walnut rail, previous-disabled/next-enabled brass arrows, 3 dots, actual Woodland/Space/Garden with eight-tone decorative family and both shelves. ## Newest WS-14E brand/shared-assets/motion design — November 9, 2026
